## Parameters

- Atlas of record: art/catalogue/catalogue.json sheets[] and entries[].slot. WG.30.01 (master sheet allocation) has no deliverable, so the "WG.30 atlas coordinate" of WSR-03 is read as the catalogue slot, confirmed in the template output.
- Art-required visual classes (WSR-02, WSR-03): VISUAL_REQUIRED, VFX_REQUIRED
- Composed exemption allowed for systems: LANDMARKS_HISTORY
- Resolution methods: ID (entry id, or TYPE field); SOURCE (bare source id); MAP (ACCEPTED rows only)
- Source classes (NON_WORLD_STATE slots must cite one of these source kinds): ADDENDUM_FAMILIES = addendum; CHARACTERS = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest; CREATURES = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest; EFFECTS = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest; ITEMS = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest; PROPS = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest; UI = catalog/assetIndex/brief/ar/manifest

## Not checkable statically

- WSR-05: States marked ACTIVE_FRONT_QUEUE or REGIONAL_DIRTY must never be scanned globally per frame. A runtime property of the simulation code. The registry, catalogue and templates do not record it, so a static check cannot see it.
